# Field Companion · Wave 3 — "The visit spine" · Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** She answers "where are you?" once at the door, and everything she captures for the next two hours lands on the right project and room — offline — with the visit legible on every capture surface (Invariant V).

**Architecture:** The visit is `CaptureSessionContext`, promoted from invisible routing memory to the app's spine: a device-minted `visitID` plus kind/kit/label/rooms/endedAt, persisted in the App Group `UserDefaults`. A new offline project+room cache extends the existing `CaptureProjectRef` `@Model` with additive optionals (no new `@Model`, no `VersionedSchema`). Every new decision rule, state builder and merge is a **pure type in CaptureKit** so it is unit-testable — `CaptureTests` links CaptureKit only and has no app host, so app-side SwiftUI is a thin renderer over a tested CaptureKit value. Server-side, the visit rides the existing `commit_field_capture` payload into `field_captures.raw_payload` and is projected into ten new columns by a **BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE trigger** — deliberately *not* a `CREATE OR REPLACE` of `commit_field_capture`, which is a shared object with a second live author (§9.0/FC-R18).

**Tech Stack:** Swift 6 / SwiftUI / SwiftData (iOS 18+), Swift Testing (`import Testing`, `#expect`), CaptureKit framework + Capture app target + CaptureTests bundle (all generated by `scripts/generate_project.rb`, which globs `**/*.swift` — a new file needs no pbxproj edit), Postgres 15 (Supabase/Strata), `psql` SQL tests via `scripts/run-sql-tests.sh`.

**Spec:** `docs/design/field-companion/field-companion-package.md` (§5 IA + launch table + Invariant V, §6 Flows 1–4/6/7, §7.1–7.11 screen catalogue — the ASCII wireframes are the UI spec, §9.3/§9.7/§9.8 data model, §13 offline/sync, §14 telemetry, §15 consent, §17 migration path). Program plan: `docs/design/field-companion/field-companion-plan.md` §3 (packages 3-1…3-12, acceptance, gates, risk register R3-1..R3-3) and §8 (Wave 1 interface block). Rulings: `docs/design/field-companion/field-companion-rulings.md`, "Ratified by Kody — 2026-08-24".

**Review:** `docs/design/field-companion/plans/wave-3-plan-review.md` (verdict READY-WITH-FIXES — 1 critical, 5 high, 12 medium, 13 low). Every finding is dispositioned below or in the task it belongs to.

---

## Rulings applied 2026-08-24

Six orchestrator rulings closed the review's findings. Each is binding on the task that carries it, and each is restated at the point of use.

> **Ruling 1 — the projection trigger never raises.** `field_captures_project_visit_columns()` parses every projected value defensively: an unknown `visit_kind` / `visit_kit` / `suggestion_basis` → NULL; an unparsable timestamp, uuid or numeric → NULL; an out-of-range `suggestion_confidence` → NULL. Each drop is recorded in `raw_payload->'visit_projection_errors'` as a jsonb array of `{key, sqlstate, at}`. **Never a RAISE, and never a CHECK violation on either the inbox or the library destination.** The named CHECK constraints stay — they remain the invariant for anything writing the columns directly — and the trigger is the guarantor that a device payload can no longer trip them. (Closes **C1**; Task 9 carries the SQL, Task 10 the six malformed-payload assertions.)

> **Ruling 2 — Task 0 gates on the MERGED Wave 1 and Wave 2, on `main`.** Not on branch snapshots. Wave 1 is landing `VoiceRecordingPolicy`, `VoiceNoteResult.audioSegments` / `.onDevice`, the `Specimen` voice fields and the `FieldCapturePayload` version bump *now*; Task 0 verifies them **after** the merge. It also **reads the actual `currentSchemaVersion` value (call it N)** and Task 8 sets it to **N + 1**. The plan's earlier "2 → 3" assumption is withdrawn: no task hardcodes `3`, and the SQL test asserts the version **round-trips** rather than equalling a literal. (Closes **H1**, **H2**.)

> **Ruling 3 — FC-R6 semantics: unplaced is `project_id == nil`, regardless of sync state.** `unfiled()` **includes `.committed` rows**. The tray and Today's *"N captures not placed yet"* line empty on **placement**, never on sync — an unplaced note waits on Today until she files it, which is the whole mechanism FC-R6 names. Placement of an already-committed capture rides the **existing** outbox — `Specimen.place(…)` flags it and the ordinary drain re-runs `commit_field_capture`, which wave 1's `00530` teaches to persist `project_id` on the inbox branch. ⚠ **Not `route_field_capture`**: that RPC ends in `RETURN commit_field_capture(…, 'library', …)` (`00235:330-338`) — the destination is hardcoded, so routing an unplaced site-visit capture through it would mint a draft library product out of a photo of a damaged baseboard. The review's suggested mechanism is rejected on that evidence; its semantics are kept in full. Under no circumstances is a second queue introduced. `CaptureStatus` has **no `.dismissed` case**; the doc comment saying otherwise is deleted. (Closes **H3**.)

> **Ruling 4 — the FC-R11 affirmation is a tappable gate on BOTH C6 and the C3 inline mic.** Same component, same behaviour: a `Button` chip backed by `@State private var affirmed`, and the record control refuses to start a `conversation` note until it is tapped. C3 is the two-taps-and-a-hold path she uses most inside a walk-through — the kit whose default *is* `conversation` — so it is the surface the ruling is most about. Inert `Text` is not an affirmation. (Closes **H5**.)

> **Ruling 5 — the remaining mediums and lows land exactly as the review specifies.** Task 6's first test binds its context to a `let` before comparing (two `visit(…)` calls mint two `visitID`s and can never compare equal); the stale-window test pins an explicit `Calendar`/`TimeZone`; `FieldLaunchPolicy.destination` takes `todayIsHome` as a defaulted parameter instead of being globally mutated by a parallel test; eviction never deletes a `CaptureProjectRef` a specimen or S1/S2 owns; `ownableProjectIDs` comes from `ownableProjects()`, not the full list; every screenshot suffix a task asserts is added to `scripts/capture-shots.sh`; C6 tracks and passes a real segment count; the tautological trigger-order assertion reads both `tgname`s from `pg_trigger`; the two untested safety properties get assertion groups; the File Structure table is completed; every drifted line number is re-derived from the working tree and the wrong filename corrected.

> **Ruling 6 — the mode selector reads `CameraMode.viewfinderSelectable`, not `allCases`.** Wave 2 introduces `viewfinderSelectable = [.photo, .tag, .measure, .scan]` as a literal and holds `.voice` off it so it does not ship a dead pill. Wave 3's Task 23 is what flips that property to admit `.voice`; the selector rows and the swipe cycle already read it, so no selector code changes. Any earlier claim in this plan that the selector "already iterates `allCases`" is withdrawn.

---

## Global Constraints

- **Patina Field is not live anywhere → no backward compatibility is owed inside the app.** `CaptureSessionContext`'s persisted `Codable` shape may change freely. **No legacy-decode test. No `capture.session-context.v1` upgrade path.** A fresh install may reset the local store. (Ruling: standing facts, 2026-08-24. This *overrides* plan package 3-2's and 3-12's "legacy-decode path is mandatory" line, which predates the ruling.)
- **The database is live prod.** Every migration is **additive and idempotent** (`ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS`, `CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS`, `CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION`), RPC signatures unchanged, RLS policies `TO authenticated`, and every **new** `public.` routine carries the explicit `REVOKE ALL … FROM PUBLIC, anon` idiom (`00437:516-529`). Per-designer scope in v1 (**FC-R8**).
- **Migration numbering:** this wave's file is written as `005NN_field_capture_visit_and_suggestion.sql`. `NN` is **drawn from the reserved band `00530–00535` at landing by the orchestrator**, after re-checking `docs/engineering/migration-number-reservations.md` **and** `supabase migration list` against Strata. **This plan's SQL lives at `docs/design/field-companion/plans/sql/`, NOT `supabase/migrations/`.** No file in this wave is named `005NN_*.sql` under `supabase/migrations/`.
- **Gates, verbatim:** `apps/mobile/Capture/scripts/capture-gate.sh all` and `swiftlint --strict`. SQL: `scripts/run-sql-tests.sh -f field_capture_visit` (standalone) **and** the full runner `scripts/run-sql-tests.sh` — **22 documented known failures are expected** (`supabase/tests/KNOWN_FAILURES.md`); the runner exits 0 if only those fail. ⚠ **The runner connects as `postgres` (superuser), so it proves the trigger's and RPC's *logic* with RLS bypassed. No task in this wave may report "RLS verified" on the strength of a SQL test.**
- **FC-R2 vocabulary:** two kinds — `site`, `sourcing`. Three kits — `walk_through`, `trade_walk`, `install`. **A kit is never also a kind.** No visit = **null kind**.
- **FC-R5 room lanes, never crossed:** `project_rooms.id` → `field_captures.project_room_id`. `public.rooms.id` → `siteScanContext.projectRoomId` provenance + `room_scans.room_id`. A merged picker entry carries up to two ids; when a room exists in only one list the other lane stays null. **Never cross-assign.** `ContextCaptureProvenance.swift:21`'s refusal stands.
- **FC-R11 consent:** a note is `solo` or `conversation`, chosen at start; the **kit carries the default** (`walk_through` → `conversation`; everything else → `solo`); a conversation note shows a one-line affirmation chip she taps; recording chrome is unmissable; never ambient. **FC-R9: no background audio.**
- **Principle 4:** `suggestion_confidence` **orders** the tray and is **never rendered**. The basis is always shown **in words**. A surface that renders the number is a bug.
- **Copy** follows `.agents/skills/patina-brand-voice/SKILL.md`: plain-spoken Midwest, sensory and specific, understatement over exclamation. **The word "Inbox" leaves Field's user-facing copy** (FC-R3). **Never the word "AI"**, never algorithm/engine/model mechanics in anything a designer reads.
- **Naming (FC-R3), adopted:** *Today* · *a visit* · *Visits* · *unplaced*.
- **There is no `CaptureType.caption`.** The enum is exactly `display, title, title2, body, bodyEmph, callout, footnote, eyebrow, monoSmall, monoBody` (`CaptureType.swift:22-35`).
- **No flag for this wave.** The gate is the build. Rollback = prior build; the migration is additive and inert without a Field build that writes the payload keys.
- **Every task ends on a green `capture-gate.sh all` + `swiftlint --strict` before its commit.**
- **The Interfaces blocks are a contract, not a sketch.** Wave 4's Task 0 reads them rather than the source, so each one carries `let` vs `var`, `@MainActor`, and the enclosing type exactly as the implementation does. Anything touching a SwiftData `@Model` (`Specimen`, `CaptureProjectRef`) is `@MainActor`; a stored `static` cannot live in an extension of a type declared in another file, so those are computed `var`s.

## Task 0 — Pre-flight re-verification against merged Waves 1–2

**Model:** haiku · **Makes no commit.** This is a gate, not a change.

> **Ruling 2 (2026-08-24): this task gates on the MERGED Wave 1 and Wave 2, on `main` — not on a branch snapshot.** Wave 1 is in flight on `feat/field-companion-w1` and is landing `VoiceRecordingPolicy`, `VoiceNoteResult.audioSegments`/`.onDevice`, the `Specimen` voice fields and the `FieldCapturePayload` version bump *now*. Verify them **after** the merge, and **read the actual `currentSchemaVersion` value** rather than asserting one.

At the time this plan was written neither wave had merged: `main` was at `27fdaf130` with **zero** Field Companion commits, `feat/field-companion-w1` was mid-wave (`VoiceRecordingPolicy` present at `CaptureKit/CaptureKit/Recognition/VoiceRecordingPolicy.swift`, `currentSchemaVersion` still `1`), and no `feat/field-companion-w2` branch existed. So every Wave 1/2 name below was taken from spec §5.5, plan §8 and `plans/wave-2-plan.md`, **not** read off `main`. Verify each one before writing a line of Wave 3.

⚠ **`plans/wave-2-plan.md` was itself reviewed NOT-READY** (`plans/wave-2-plan-review.md:9` — one critical, four high). Wave 3 consumes `CaptureFeatureFlags` + `AppContainer.featureFlags` (Tasks 20, 23), `CameraMode.viewfinderSelectable` (Task 23), the four `CaptureScreenID` cases and `CaptureSheet.visit` from it. Confirm those landed in the shape wave 2 finally shipped, not the shape its draft promised.

**Files:** none (read-only).

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Wave 1 — `CaptureAnalytics.isFeatureEnabled(_:)`, `CaptureMediaMime`, `VoiceRecordingPolicy`, `CaptureRoutingMemory.stamped(onto:)`, `VoiceNoteResult.audioSegments`/`.onDevice`, `Specimen.voiceAudioSegmentsRaw`/`.captureKindRaw`/`.voiceTranscriptSourceRaw`, `FieldCapturePayload.Voice.audioSegments`/`.transcriptSource`/`.noteSetting`, `FieldCapturePayload.captureKind`, `FieldCapturePayload.currentSchemaVersion` (**value read, not asserted**). Wave 2 — `CaptureSheet.visit` (registryKey `"visit"`), `CameraMode.voice` + `CameraMode.viewfinderSelectable`, `CaptureScreenID.v0Visit` (`screen.V0.visit`) / `.c6Voice` (`screen.C6.voice`) / `.v4VisitReview` (`screen.V4.visit-review`) / `.f1Context` (`screen.F1.context`) + `.sweepSuffix`, `AppContainer.smartGuess`, `AppContainer.featureFlags: CaptureFeatureFlags` with `.isEnabled(_:)` / `.allOff` and the key `"field-companion-voice"`, `SpecimenCapturePolicy.nextStep(for: .voice) == .quickConfirm`, `SmartGuess.fieldsWorthRecording`, `Specimen.confidence(for:)`, `SmartGuessConfidence.confirmedFloor`, `CaptureCoordinator.siteScanContextRequested`, `CaptureSyncAttributes.ContentState.visitLabel`/`.elapsedSeconds`/`.captureCount`.
- Produces: a verified name list every later task in this plan depends on, **and the number `N` that Task 8 bumps.**

Do not code a fix or a workaround here — add it as an explicit assertion in **Task 33's device-pass table** (a fast-tap Save immediately after the shutter, on a real device, checking whether the category still lands) rather than inventing a code-level guard for a race no test has reproduced. |
| 3 | The `.dimensions` F-1-class hole: `setValue` `break`s on `.dimensions` without writing a value but still stamps `provenanceRaw` and calls `touch()` outside the switch, so a future `.dimensions` guess would stamp guess provenance on a value nobody wrote. Unreachable today — `HeuristicSmartGuessService` emits only `.category`/`.material`/`.colorway`. | **No wave-3 task touches `Specimen+Accessors.swift`'s `setValue` or emits `.dimensions`.** The one-line fix (`guard suggestion.key != .dimensions` in `fieldsWorthRecording`) stays owed; re-carry until a wave changes either file. |
| 4 | Keyword shadowing in `SmartGuessKeywords.swift`: first-match-wins substring matching sends `"table lamp"` / `"desk lamp"` to `.table` instead of `.lighting`, and `"tapestry"` to `.plumbing`. `everyKeywordInTheTableResolvesToItsOwnCategory` only pins the table against itself — no test covers the label space where the defect actually bites. | **No wave-3 task touches `SmartGuessKeywords.swift`.** Owed: a label-space test (assert `"table lamp"`, `"desk lamp"`, `"tapestry"` against their *intended* category, not just against the table's own keys) before anyone relies on the ordering being correct rather than merely locked. |

| 6 | `-CaptureScreen voice` harness-token ambiguity (M1): with `screen.C6.voice` joining `screen.N4.voice` this wave (Task 23), the bare token `voice` still resolves to `.n4Voice` by declaration order — unchanged behaviour, but now ambiguous to a human typing it by hand. Automated sweeps are unaffected because they always pass full suffixes (`C6.voice`, per Task 23 Step 4). | **Task 23 uses the full-suffix form (`-CaptureScreen C6.voice`) and does not resolve the bare-token ambiguity.** Still owed as a harness clarity item — not a functional bug — re-carry to whichever wave next touches the `-CaptureScreen` argument parser. |
| 7 | `CaptureScreenIDTests.everyScreenIDIsUnique` compares full remaining suffixes, not shared trailing components — `screen.N4.voice` vs `screen.C6.voice` share only the trailing `.voice` segment and the test does not catch that class of collision. Latent in wave 2 (`c6Voice` was reserved and unused); becomes live the moment Task 23 ships C6 as a real screen alongside N4. | **Task 23** is where this stops being latent — its report should note the test gap explicitly (not just add the new case) so the next screen-id addition doesn't assume `everyScreenIDIsUnique` guards more than it does. |

```swift
// CaptureKit/CaptureKit/Domain/FieldVisit.swift
public struct CaptureCachedRoom: Codable, Hashable, Sendable {
    public let id: String
    public let name: String
    public init(id: String, name: String)
}
public struct CaptureCoordinate: Codable, Hashable, Sendable {
    public let latitude: Double
    public let longitude: Double
    public init(latitude: Double, longitude: Double)
    /// Great-circle distance in metres (spherical earth, R = 6_371_000 m).
    public func distanceMeters(to other: CaptureCoordinate) -> Double
}

// CaptureKit/CaptureKit/Domain/Specimen.swift — CaptureProjectRef, all additive optionals
public var specRoomsData: Data?          // JSON [CaptureCachedRoom] — project_rooms lane
public var roomsData: Data?              // JSON [CaptureCachedRoom] — public.rooms lane
public var lastRefreshedAt: Date?
public var lastVisitedAt: Date?
public var lastFiledLatitude: Double?
public var lastFiledLongitude: Double?
public var filedCaptureCount: Int?
public var specRooms: [CaptureCachedRoom] { get set }        // computed over specRoomsData
public var rooms: [CaptureCachedRoom] { get set }            // computed over roomsData
public var lastFiledCoordinate: CaptureCoordinate? { get set } // computed over the two Doubles
```

⚠ `lastFiledCoordinate` is a **computed pair of `Double?` columns**, not a stored tuple — SwiftData cannot persist a tuple, and the two lists are JSON `Data?` rather than a stored `[CaptureCachedRoom]` so no composite-type migration is involved. All seven stored properties are optional, so SwiftData migrates lightweight and **no `VersionedSchema` is introduced** (`Specimen.swift:7` reserves that for the foundation owner).

```swift
//  FieldVisit.swift
//  CaptureKit
//
//  The visit vocabulary (FC-R2) and the small value types the offline cache,
//  the room merge (FC-R5) and the suggestion lane all share.

import Foundation

/// FC-R2: two kinds. "Roving" is the ABSENCE of a kind, never a case.
public enum FieldVisitKind: String, Codable, CaseIterable, Sendable {
    case site
    case sourcing
}

/// FC-R2: three kits, and a kit is never also a kind.
public enum FieldVisitKit: String, Codable, CaseIterable, Sendable {
    case walkThrough = "walk_through"
    case tradeWalk   = "trade_walk"
    case install
}

/// FC-R11: chosen at the start of every note; the kit carries the default.
public enum FieldNoteSetting: String, Codable, CaseIterable, Sendable {
    case solo
    case conversation
}

/// §9.3 `suggestion_basis`. Waves 1–3 produce only `visit`, `venue` and `proximity`.
public enum FieldSuggestionBasis: String, Codable, CaseIterable, Sendable {
    case visit, scan, proximity, venue, calendar, transcript
}

public struct CaptureCachedRoom: Codable, Hashable, Sendable {
    public let id: String
    public let name: String
    public init(id: String, name: String) {
        self.id = id
        self.name = name
    }
}

public struct CaptureCoordinate: Codable, Hashable, Sendable {
    public let latitude: Double
    public let longitude: Double

    public init(latitude: Double, longitude: Double) {
        self.latitude = latitude
        self.longitude = longitude
    }

    /// Great-circle distance in metres over a spherical earth. CoreLocation is
    /// app-side; CaptureKit stays pure Foundation so this unit-tests.
    public func distanceMeters(to other: CaptureCoordinate) -> Double {
        let earthRadius = 6_371_000.0
        let lat1 = latitude * .pi / 180
        let lat2 = other.latitude * .pi / 180
        let dLat = (other.latitude - latitude) * .pi / 180
        let dLon = (other.longitude - longitude) * .pi / 180
        let a = sin(dLat / 2) * sin(dLat / 2)
            + cos(lat1) * cos(lat2) * sin(dLon / 2) * sin(dLon / 2)
        return earthRadius * 2 * atan2(sqrt(a), sqrt(max(0, 1 - a)))
    }
}
```

In `apps/mobile/Capture/CaptureKit/CaptureKit/Domain/Specimen.swift`, inside `CaptureProjectRef` (after `ownerWorkspaceID`, before `init`), add:

```swift
    // enters the schema. lastFiledCoordinate is a computed pair: SwiftData
    // cannot persist a tuple.
```

and after `belongs(to:)` add:

```swift
    public var specRooms: [CaptureCachedRoom] {
        get { CaptureProjectRef.decodeRooms(specRoomsData) }
        set { specRoomsData = CaptureProjectRef.encodeRooms(newValue) }
    }

    public var rooms: [CaptureCachedRoom] {
        get { CaptureProjectRef.decodeRooms(roomsData) }
        set { roomsData = CaptureProjectRef.encodeRooms(newValue) }
    }

    public var lastFiledCoordinate: CaptureCoordinate? {
        get {
            guard let lastFiledLatitude, let lastFiledLongitude else { return nil }
            return CaptureCoordinate(latitude: lastFiledLatitude, longitude: lastFiledLongitude)
        }
        set {
            lastFiledLatitude = newValue?.latitude
            lastFiledLongitude = newValue?.longitude
        }
    }

    private static func decodeRooms(_ data: Data?) -> [CaptureCachedRoom] {
        guard let data else { return [] }
        return (try? JSONDecoder().decode([CaptureCachedRoom].self, from: data)) ?? []
    }

    private static func encodeRooms(_ rooms: [CaptureCachedRoom]) -> Data? {
        rooms.isEmpty ? nil : try? JSONEncoder().encode(rooms)
    }
```

```swift
public struct CaptureProjectSnapshot: Identifiable, Hashable, Sendable {
    /// `projects.id` when the ref has one, else `CaptureProjectRef.id.uuidString` —
    /// the same fallback `S2CreateProjectScreen.swift:162` already writes into
    /// `routing.projectID` for a project created offline.

    public let name: String

    public let lastVisitedAt: Date?
    public let lastFiledCoordinate: CaptureCoordinate?
    public let filedCaptureCount: Int
    /// True when `id` is a LOCAL uuid, not a `projects.id`. Selectable, captionable,
    /// and not yet routable server-side.
    public let isAwaitingSync: Bool
    public init(id: String, name: String, specRooms: [CaptureCachedRoom],
                rooms: [CaptureCachedRoom], lastRefreshedAt: Date?, lastVisitedAt: Date?,
                lastFiledCoordinate: CaptureCoordinate?, filedCaptureCount: Int,
                isAwaitingSync: Bool = false)
    /// True when the row has never been refreshed, or was refreshed before `now - staleAfter`.
    public func isStale(now: Date) -> Bool
}

public enum CaptureProjectCachePolicy {
    public static let staleAfter: TimeInterval          // 7 * 24 * 60 * 60
    public static let evictAfter: TimeInterval          // 60 * 24 * 60 * 60
    public static let maxCachedProjects: Int            // 60
    /// Recent-first: most recently visited, then most recently refreshed, then name.
    public static func ordered(_ snapshots: [CaptureProjectSnapshot], now: Date) -> [CaptureProjectSnapshot]
    /// Which cached rows to delete. Never evicts a project visited inside `evictAfter`.
    public static func evictable(_ snapshots: [CaptureProjectSnapshot], now: Date) -> [String]
    /// §13's specified failure copy. Never "no projects", never a spinner.
    public static func offlineCaption(cachedCount: Int) -> String
    public static func filter(_ snapshots: [CaptureProjectSnapshot], query: String) -> [CaptureProjectSnapshot]
}
```

```swift
//  CaptureProjectCache.swift
//  CaptureKit
//
//  The offline project + room cache (spec §13.3). The door MUST work offline:
//  never an empty list, never a spinner, never a disabled control.

import Foundation

public struct CaptureProjectSnapshot: Identifiable, Hashable, Sendable {
    public let id: String
    public let name: String
    public let specRooms: [CaptureCachedRoom]
    public let rooms: [CaptureCachedRoom]
    public let lastRefreshedAt: Date?
    public let lastVisitedAt: Date?
    public let lastFiledCoordinate: CaptureCoordinate?
    public let filedCaptureCount: Int
    /// True when `id` is a LOCAL uuid because the project has not synced yet.
    public let isAwaitingSync: Bool

    public init(id: String, name: String,
                specRooms: [CaptureCachedRoom], rooms: [CaptureCachedRoom],
                lastRefreshedAt: Date?, lastVisitedAt: Date?,
                lastFiledCoordinate: CaptureCoordinate?, filedCaptureCount: Int,
                isAwaitingSync: Bool = false) {
        self.id = id
        self.name = name
        self.specRooms = specRooms
        self.rooms = rooms
        self.lastRefreshedAt = lastRefreshedAt
        self.lastVisitedAt = lastVisitedAt
        self.lastFiledCoordinate = lastFiledCoordinate
        self.filedCaptureCount = filedCaptureCount
        self.isAwaitingSync = isAwaitingSync
    }

    public func isStale(now: Date) -> Bool {
        guard let lastRefreshedAt else { return true }
        return now.timeIntervalSince(lastRefreshedAt) > CaptureProjectCachePolicy.staleAfter
    }
}

public enum CaptureProjectCachePolicy {
    public static let staleAfter: TimeInterval = 7 * 24 * 60 * 60
    public static let evictAfter: TimeInterval = 60 * 24 * 60 * 60
    public static let maxCachedProjects = 60

    public static func ordered(_ snapshots: [CaptureProjectSnapshot],
                               now: Date) -> [CaptureProjectSnapshot] {
        snapshots.sorted { lhs, rhs in
            let l = lhs.lastVisitedAt ?? lhs.lastRefreshedAt ?? .distantPast
            let r = rhs.lastVisitedAt ?? rhs.lastRefreshedAt ?? .distantPast
            if l != r { return l > r }
            return lhs.name.localizedCaseInsensitiveCompare(rhs.name) == .orderedAscending
        }
    }

    public static func evictable(_ snapshots: [CaptureProjectSnapshot],
                                 now: Date) -> [String] {
        let expired = snapshots.filter { snapshot in
            let touched = snapshot.lastVisitedAt ?? snapshot.lastRefreshedAt ?? .distantPast
            return now.timeIntervalSince(touched) > evictAfter
        }
        let overflow = ordered(snapshots, now: now).dropFirst(maxCachedProjects)
        var ids = Set(expired.map(\.id))
        ids.formUnion(overflow.map(\.id))
        return ordered(snapshots, now: now).map(\.id).filter { ids.contains($0) }
    }

    public static func offlineCaption(cachedCount: Int) -> String {
        switch cachedCount {
        case 0:  return "No projects on this phone yet. They arrive with signal."
        case 1:  return "1 project on this phone. Others need signal."
        default: return "\(cachedCount) projects on this phone. Others need signal."
        }
    }

    public static func filter(_ snapshots: [CaptureProjectSnapshot],
                              query: String) -> [CaptureProjectSnapshot] {
        let needle = query.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines)
        guard !needle.isEmpty else { return snapshots }
        return snapshots.filter { $0.name.localizedCaseInsensitiveContains(needle) }
    }
}
```

### Task 3: The cache itself — owner-scoped read, refresh and filed-coordinate learning

**Model:** opus

**Files:**
- Modify: `apps/mobile/Capture/CaptureKit/CaptureKit/Work/CaptureProjectCache.swift`
- Test: `apps/mobile/Capture/CaptureTests/ProjectCacheTests.swift` (extend)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `CaptureStore` (`Persistence/CaptureStore.swift`), `CaptureProjectRef`, `ProjectsService` / `FieldProject` / `FieldProjectDetail` / `FieldProjectRoom` (`Work/ProjectsService.swift`), `CaptureOwnerIdentity`, `CaptureProjectSnapshot` (Task 2).
- Produces:
```swift
@MainActor
public final class CaptureProjectCache {
    public init(store: CaptureStore, projects: any ProjectsService)
    /// Cached projects for this owner, recent-first. NEVER throws, NEVER blocks on
    /// network, and NEVER hides a project she created offline.
    public func snapshots(owner: CaptureOwnerIdentity, now: Date = Date()) -> [CaptureProjectSnapshot]
    /// Best-effort refresh of the list. Returns false when the network refused;
    /// the caller keeps rendering the cache and shows `offlineCaption`.
    @discardableResult
    public func refreshList(owner: CaptureOwnerIdentity, now: Date = Date()) async -> Bool
    /// Best-effort refresh of one project's two room lanes (FC-R5).
    @discardableResult
    public func refreshDetail(projectID: String, owner: CaptureOwnerIdentity, now: Date = Date()) async -> Bool
    /// Called when a capture is FILED to a project — feeds the learned centroid.
    public func recordFiling(projectID: String, at coordinate: CaptureCoordinate?,
                             owner: CaptureOwnerIdentity, now: Date = Date())
    /// Called when a visit opens on a project.
    public func recordVisit(projectID: String, owner: CaptureOwnerIdentity, now: Date = Date())
}
```

⚠ `recordFiling` keeps a **running mean** of filed coordinates per project — that is the learned centroid (§2.2). It never writes to `Specimen` and never touches the network.

⚠ **`CaptureProjectRef` is NOT a cache-only model, and this task must not treat it as one.** `S2CreateProjectScreen.swift:148` creates rows and `S1AssignVenueScreen.swift:248-265` fetches them for the venue picker. Two consequences, both load-bearing:

1. **Eviction may only ever delete rows the cache itself created, and never one anything references.** The guard is two-part: a row is a candidate only if `lastRefreshedAt != nil` (the cache wrote it), and it is spared outright if any `Specimen.venue?.projectId` in the store names it. Deleting a designer's locally-created project list as "cache maintenance" is a data-loss bug, not a policy.
2. **A project created offline has no `remoteId` until it syncs, and it must still appear on the door** — the one screen this wave insists works in airplane mode. `snapshot(_:)` therefore falls back to `ref.id.uuidString`, which is exactly what the shipped `S2CreateProjectScreen.swift:162` already writes into `routing.projectID` in the same situation (`remoteId ?? project.id.uuidString`). The snapshot carries `isAwaitingSync` so the door can caption it honestly; the capture filed to it stays **unplaced server-side** until the project syncs, which is precisely the state FC-R6 says waits on Today.

```swift
        #expect(snap.filedCaptureCount == 2)

        // Exactly what S2CreateProjectScreen writes when the create call fails:
        // a ref with a name, an owner and NO remoteId.

        store.context.insert(local)
        try store.save()

    @Test func evictionNeverDeletesARowTheCacheDidNotCreate() async throws {
        let store = try CaptureStore.inMemory()
        let owner = CaptureOwnerIdentity(userID: "u1", workspaceID: "w1")!
        let now = Date(timeIntervalSince1970: 1_800_000_000)
        let ancient = now.addingTimeInterval(-CaptureProjectCachePolicy.evictAfter - 1)

        // (a) S2's row: created locally, never refreshed by the cache.
        let s2Row = CaptureProjectRef(remoteId: "p-local", name: "Kippley", owner: owner)
        s2Row.lastVisitedAt = ancient
        // (b) A cache row that IS old enough to evict, but a capture points at it.
        let referenced = CaptureProjectRef(remoteId: "p-referenced", name: "Harbor", owner: owner)
        referenced.lastRefreshedAt = ancient
        // (c) A cache row nothing references — the only legitimate eviction.
        let orphan = CaptureProjectRef(remoteId: "p-orphan", name: "Old job", owner: owner)
        orphan.lastRefreshedAt = ancient
        for ref in [s2Row, referenced, orphan] { store.context.insert(ref) }

        let capture = store.newDraft(owner: owner)
        capture.venue = VenueStamp(projectId: "p-referenced", projectName: "Harbor")
        try store.save()

        let service = StubProjectsService()
        service.list = [FieldProject(id: "p1", name: "Maple St", status: "active")]
        let cache = CaptureProjectCache(store: store, projects: service)
        _ = await cache.refreshList(owner: owner, now: now)

        let survivors = Set(cache.snapshots(owner: owner, now: now).map(\.id))
        #expect(survivors.contains("p-local"))       // S2 owns it; the cache must not
        #expect(survivors.contains("p-referenced"))  // a capture names it
        #expect(!survivors.contains("p-orphan"))     // this one, and only this one
    }
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the implementation**

Append to `apps/mobile/Capture/CaptureKit/CaptureKit/Work/CaptureProjectCache.swift`:

```swift
import SwiftData

@MainActor
public final class CaptureProjectCache {
    private let store: CaptureStore
    private let projects: any ProjectsService

    public init(store: CaptureStore, projects: any ProjectsService) {
        self.store = store
        self.projects = projects
    }

    public func snapshots(owner: CaptureOwnerIdentity,
                          now: Date = Date()) -> [CaptureProjectSnapshot] {
        CaptureProjectCachePolicy.ordered(refs(owner: owner).map(Self.snapshot), now: now)
    }

    @discardableResult
    public func refreshList(owner: CaptureOwnerIdentity, now: Date = Date()) async -> Bool {
        let remote: [FieldProject]
        do {
            remote = try await projects.listProjects()
        } catch {
            return false
        }
        for project in remote {
            let ref = upsert(remoteID: project.id, name: project.name, owner: owner)
            ref.name = project.name
            ref.lastRefreshedAt = now
        }
        evict(owner: owner, now: now)
        try? store.save()
        return true
    }

    @discardableResult
    public func refreshDetail(projectID: String, owner: CaptureOwnerIdentity,
                              now: Date = Date()) async -> Bool {
        let detail: FieldProjectDetail
        do {
            detail = try await projects.projectDetail(id: projectID)
        } catch {
            return false
        }
        let ref = upsert(remoteID: projectID, name: detail.project.name, owner: owner)
        // FC-R5: the two lanes are stored SEPARATELY and never cross-assigned.
        ref.specRooms = detail.specRooms.map { CaptureCachedRoom(id: $0.id, name: $0.name) }
        ref.rooms = detail.rooms.map { CaptureCachedRoom(id: $0.id, name: $0.name) }
        ref.lastRefreshedAt = now
        try? store.save()
        return true
    }

    public func recordFiling(projectID: String, at coordinate: CaptureCoordinate?,
                             owner: CaptureOwnerIdentity, now: Date = Date()) {
        guard let ref = ref(snapshotID: projectID, owner: owner) else { return }
        let previousCount = Double(ref.filedCaptureCount ?? 0)
        ref.filedCaptureCount = (ref.filedCaptureCount ?? 0) + 1
        if let coordinate {
            if let existing = ref.lastFiledCoordinate, previousCount > 0 {
                let total = previousCount + 1
                ref.lastFiledCoordinate = CaptureCoordinate(
                    latitude: (existing.latitude * previousCount + coordinate.latitude) / total,
                    longitude: (existing.longitude * previousCount + coordinate.longitude) / total)
            } else {
                ref.lastFiledCoordinate = coordinate
            }
        }
        try? store.save()
    }

    public func recordVisit(projectID: String, owner: CaptureOwnerIdentity, now: Date = Date()) {
        guard let ref = ref(snapshotID: projectID, owner: owner) else { return }
        ref.lastVisitedAt = now
        try? store.save()
    }

    // ── plumbing ──

    private func refs(owner: CaptureOwnerIdentity) -> [CaptureProjectRef] {
        let all = (try? store.context.fetch(FetchDescriptor<CaptureProjectRef>())) ?? []
        // NO remoteId filter: a project created offline (S2, with the create call
        // refused) has none until it syncs, and the door must still list it.
        return all.filter { $0.belongs(to: owner) }
    }

    private func ref(snapshotID: String, owner: CaptureOwnerIdentity) -> CaptureProjectRef? {
        refs(owner: owner).first { Self.snapshotID($0) == snapshotID }
    }

    private func upsert(remoteID: String, name: String,
                        owner: CaptureOwnerIdentity) -> CaptureProjectRef {
        if let existing = refs(owner: owner).first(where: { $0.remoteId == remoteID }) {
            return existing
        }
        let ref = CaptureProjectRef(remoteId: remoteID, name: name, owner: owner)
        store.context.insert(ref)
        return ref
    }

    /// Deletes ONLY rows this cache created and nothing points at. `CaptureProjectRef`
    /// is also S2's inline-created-project record and S1's venue picker's list
    /// (`S2CreateProjectScreen.swift:148`, `S1AssignVenueScreen.swift:248-265`), so a
    /// blanket delete-by-age would take the designer's own project list with it.
    private func evict(owner: CaptureOwnerIdentity, now: Date) {
        // (1) Only rows the cache itself wrote are candidates at all.
        let candidates = refs(owner: owner).filter { $0.lastRefreshedAt != nil }
        guard !candidates.isEmpty else { return }
        // (2) And never one a capture names. venue.projectId is a plain String, so
        //     there is no SwiftData relationship to protect this for us.
        let referenced = Set(
            ((try? store.context.fetch(FetchDescriptor<Specimen>())) ?? [])
                .compactMap { $0.venue?.projectId }
                .filter { !$0.isEmpty })
        let doomed = Set(CaptureProjectCachePolicy.evictable(
            candidates.map(Self.snapshot), now: now))
        guard !doomed.isEmpty else { return }
        for ref in candidates {
            let id = Self.snapshotID(ref)
            guard doomed.contains(id), !referenced.contains(id) else { continue }
            store.context.delete(ref)
        }
    }

    /// `projects.id` when there is one, else the LOCAL uuid — the same fallback
    /// S2CreateProjectScreen.swift:162 already uses for `routing.projectID`.
    private static func snapshotID(_ ref: CaptureProjectRef) -> String {
        if let remoteID = ref.remoteId, !remoteID.isEmpty { return remoteID }
        return ref.id.uuidString
    }

    private static func snapshot(_ ref: CaptureProjectRef) -> CaptureProjectSnapshot? {
        CaptureProjectSnapshot(
            id: snapshotID(ref),
            name: ref.name,
            specRooms: ref.specRooms,
            rooms: ref.rooms,
            lastRefreshedAt: ref.lastRefreshedAt,
            lastVisitedAt: ref.lastVisitedAt,
            lastFiledCoordinate: ref.lastFiledCoordinate,
            filedCaptureCount: ref.filedCaptureCount ?? 0,
            isAwaitingSync: (ref.remoteId ?? "").isEmpty)
    }
}
```

⚠ `snapshot(_:)` is now total — a ref with no `remoteId` produces a snapshot rather than vanishing — so the two call sites use `map`, not `compactMap`. `compactMap` over a non-optional transform is deprecated in Swift and would warn.

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the implementation**

Create `apps/mobile/Capture/CaptureKit/CaptureKit/Work/FieldVisitRoomMerge.swift`:

```swift
//  FieldVisitRoomMerge.swift
//  CaptureKit
//
//  FC-R5. `FieldProjectDetail` returns BOTH lists from one projectDetail(id:)
//  call: specRooms (project_rooms — what field_captures.project_room_id FKs to)
//  and rooms (public.rooms — what room_scans.room_id and the siteScanContext
//  provenance carry). One picker merges them by case-insensitive trimmed name.
//  A capture stamps ONLY the id legal for its lane; the other stays nil.
//  ContextCaptureProvenance's refusal to put a rooms.id in project_room_id stands.

import Foundation

public struct FieldVisitRoomOption: Identifiable, Hashable, Sendable {
    public let name: String
    public let projectRoomID: String?
    public let scanRoomID: String?

    public init(name: String, projectRoomID: String?, scanRoomID: String?) {
        self.name = name
        self.projectRoomID = projectRoomID
        self.scanRoomID = scanRoomID
    }

    public var id: String {
        "\(projectRoomID ?? "-")|\(scanRoomID ?? "-")|\(FieldVisitRoomMerge.normalized(name))"
    }

    public var isWholeHouse: Bool {
        projectRoomID == nil && scanRoomID == nil
            && FieldVisitRoomMerge.normalized(name) == FieldVisitRoomMerge.normalized("Whole house")
    }

    public static let wholeHouse = FieldVisitRoomOption(
        name: "Whole house", projectRoomID: nil, scanRoomID: nil)
}

public enum FieldVisitRoomMerge {

    public static func normalized(_ name: String) -> String {
        name.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines).lowercased()
    }

    public static func merge(specRooms: [CaptureCachedRoom],
                             rooms: [CaptureCachedRoom]) -> [FieldVisitRoomOption] {
        var order: [String] = []
        var byKey: [String: FieldVisitRoomOption] = [:]

        for room in specRooms {
            let key = normalized(room.name)
            guard !key.isEmpty else { continue }
            if byKey[key] == nil { order.append(key) }
            let display = room.name.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines)
            byKey[key] = FieldVisitRoomOption(name: display,
                                              projectRoomID: room.id,
                                              scanRoomID: byKey[key]?.scanRoomID)
        }
        for room in rooms {
            let key = normalized(room.name)
            guard !key.isEmpty else { continue }
            if byKey[key] == nil { order.append(key) }
            let display = byKey[key]?.name
                ?? room.name.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines)
            byKey[key] = FieldVisitRoomOption(name: display,
                                              projectRoomID: byKey[key]?.projectRoomID,
                                              scanRoomID: room.id)
        }
        return order.compactMap { byKey[$0] }
    }

    /// Spec §9.7: `FieldProjectDetail.rooms` comes from `fetchClientRooms(clientID:)`,
    /// which returns [] when a project has no registered client — so the picker
    /// silently degrades to project_rooms-only and a site scan has no room to
    /// attach to. Say it out loud rather than degrade quietly.
    public static func scanLaneCaption(_ options: [FieldVisitRoomOption]) -> String? {
        guard !options.isEmpty else { return nil }
        guard options.allSatisfy({ $0.scanRoomID == nil }) else { return nil }
        return "No client rooms on this project yet — a scan has nothing to attach to."
    }
}
```

```swift
public struct CaptureSessionContext: Codable, Equatable, Sendable {
    public let visitID: UUID
    public let identity: CaptureSessionIdentity
    public let startedAt: Date
    public var lastActivityAt: Date
    public var routing: CaptureRoutingMemory
    // ── the visit (wave 3) ──

    public var kit: FieldVisitKit?
    public var label: String?                 // "Maple St residence" | "High Point 214"
    public var scanRoomID: String?            // public.rooms.id — the SCAN lane only
    public var projectsInMind: [String]       // sourcing only, capped at 4
    public var endedAt: Date?
    public init(visitID: UUID = UUID(), identity: CaptureSessionIdentity,
                startedAt: Date, lastActivityAt: Date,
                routing: CaptureRoutingMemory = .empty,
                kind: FieldVisitKind? = nil, kit: FieldVisitKit? = nil,
                label: String? = nil, scanRoomID: String? = nil,
                projectsInMind: [String] = [], endedAt: Date? = nil)
    /// FC-R2: a context with no kind is not a visit.
    public var isVisit: Bool { kind != nil && endedAt == nil }
    /// Declared INSIDE the struct, not in an extension — Step 3 implements it there.
    public static let maxProjectsInMind = 4
}
```

```swift
public struct CaptureSessionContext: Codable, Equatable, Sendable {
    public let visitID: UUID
    public let identity: CaptureSessionIdentity
    public let startedAt: Date
    public var lastActivityAt: Date
    public var routing: CaptureRoutingMemory

    // ── The visit (wave 3). FC-R2: nil kind IS the "no visit" state. ──
    public var kind: FieldVisitKind?
    public var kit: FieldVisitKit?
    /// The visit's human label — the project name on a site visit, the venue on
    /// a sourcing run. Lands in `field_captures.visit_label`.
    public var label: String?
    /// FC-R5 SCAN lane only: a `public.rooms` id. NEVER stamped into
    /// `field_captures.project_room_id` — that is `routing.projectRoomID`.
    public var scanRoomID: String?
    /// Sourcing only, capped at `maxProjectsInMind`.
    public var projectsInMind: [String]
    public var endedAt: Date?

    public init(
        visitID: UUID = UUID(),
        identity: CaptureSessionIdentity,
        startedAt: Date,
        lastActivityAt: Date,
        routing: CaptureRoutingMemory = .empty,
        kind: FieldVisitKind? = nil,
        kit: FieldVisitKit? = nil,
        label: String? = nil,
        scanRoomID: String? = nil,
        projectsInMind: [String] = [],
        endedAt: Date? = nil
    ) {
        self.visitID = visitID
        self.identity = identity
        self.startedAt = startedAt
        self.lastActivityAt = lastActivityAt
        self.routing = routing
        self.kind = kind
        self.kit = kit
        self.label = label
        self.scanRoomID = scanRoomID
        self.projectsInMind = Array(projectsInMind.prefix(Self.maxProjectsInMind))
        self.endedAt = endedAt
    }

    public var isVisit: Bool { kind != nil && endedAt == nil }

    public static let maxProjectsInMind = 4
}
```

```swift
public enum CaptureVisitState: Equatable, Sendable {
    case none
    case active(CaptureSessionContext)
    /// Open, but untouched for longer than `staleConfirmWindow`. The UI asks
    /// "Still at Maple St?" — Resume / End visit. R3-1's headline mitigation.
    case stale(CaptureSessionContext)
    public var context: CaptureSessionContext? { get }
    public var isVisit: Bool { get }
}

public struct CaptureVisitDraft: Equatable, Sendable {
    public var kind: FieldVisitKind
    public var kit: FieldVisitKit?
    public var label: String?
    public var projectID: String?
    public var projectName: String?
    public var projectRoomID: String?    // project_rooms.id  — the CAPTURE lane
    public var scanRoomID: String?       // public.rooms.id   — the SCAN lane
    public var room: String?
    public var projectsInMind: [String]

                projectID: String? = nil, projectName: String? = nil,
                projectRoomID: String? = nil, scanRoomID: String? = nil,
                room: String? = nil, projectsInMind: [String] = [])
    /// FC-R11: the kit carries the consent default.
    public var defaultNoteSetting: FieldNoteSetting { get }
}

public extension CaptureSessionContextPolicy {
    // Computed `static var`, not `static let`: a stored static property is not
    // allowed on an extension of a type declared in another file.
    static var staleConfirmWindow: TimeInterval { get }   // 30 * 60
    static var autoEndWindow: TimeInterval { get }        // 12 * 60 * 60
    static func visitState(for context: CaptureSessionContext?, now: Date,
                           calendar: Calendar) -> CaptureVisitState
    static func started(_ draft: CaptureVisitDraft, identity: CaptureSessionIdentity,
                        now: Date) -> CaptureSessionContext
    static func ended(_ context: CaptureSessionContext, now: Date) -> CaptureSessionContext
}

public extension CaptureSessionContextStore {
    func visitState(identity: CaptureSessionIdentity, now: Date = Date(),
                    calendar: Calendar = .current) -> CaptureVisitState
    @discardableResult
    func startVisit(_ draft: CaptureVisitDraft, identity: CaptureSessionIdentity,
                    now: Date = Date()) -> CaptureSessionContext
    /// CHANGED CONTRACT: stamps `endedAt` and persists the ENDED context instead
    /// of replacing it with a fresh one. `visitState` then reads `.none`, and the
    /// next capture mints a kindless context. V1's existing call site keeps working.
    @discardableResult
    func endVisit(identity: CaptureSessionIdentity, now: Date = Date()) -> CaptureSessionContext
}
```

Create `apps/mobile/Capture/CaptureKit/CaptureKit/Session/CaptureVisitPolicy.swift`:

```swift
//  CaptureVisitPolicy.swift
//  CaptureKit
//
//  R3-1: a wrong visit is a SYSTEMATIC error — yesterday's visit silently
//  stamping today's twenty captures is worse than today's twenty unattached
//  ones. Three mitigations live here: the 30-minute confirm, the 12-hour
//  auto-end, and the rule that a visit never resumes across a calendar day.

import Foundation

public enum CaptureVisitState: Equatable, Sendable {
    case none
    case active(CaptureSessionContext)
    case stale(CaptureSessionContext)

    public var context: CaptureSessionContext? {
        switch self {
        case .none: return nil
        case .active(let c), .stale(let c): return c
        }
    }

    public var isVisit: Bool { context != nil }
}

public struct CaptureVisitDraft: Equatable, Sendable {
    public var kind: FieldVisitKind
    public var kit: FieldVisitKit?
    public var label: String?
    public var projectID: String?
    public var projectName: String?
    /// project_rooms.id — the CAPTURE lane (field_captures.project_room_id).
    public var projectRoomID: String?
    /// public.rooms.id — the SCAN lane (room_scans.room_id + provenance).
    public var scanRoomID: String?
    public var room: String?
    public var projectsInMind: [String]

    public init(kind: FieldVisitKind, kit: FieldVisitKit? = nil, label: String? = nil,
                projectID: String? = nil, projectName: String? = nil,
                projectRoomID: String? = nil, scanRoomID: String? = nil,
                room: String? = nil, projectsInMind: [String] = []) {
        self.kind = kind
        self.kit = kit
        self.label = label
        self.projectID = projectID
        self.projectName = projectName
        self.projectRoomID = projectRoomID
        self.scanRoomID = scanRoomID
        self.room = room
        self.projectsInMind = projectsInMind
    }

    /// FC-R11: a walk-through is the kit with a client in the room.
    public var defaultNoteSetting: FieldNoteSetting {
        kit == .walkThrough ? .conversation : .solo
    }
}

public extension CaptureSessionContextPolicy {
    static var staleConfirmWindow: TimeInterval { 30 * 60 }
    static var autoEndWindow: TimeInterval { 12 * 60 * 60 }

    static func visitState(for context: CaptureSessionContext?,
                           now: Date,
                           calendar: Calendar) -> CaptureVisitState {
        guard let context, context.kind != nil, context.endedAt == nil else { return .none }
        // A BACKWARDS clock (a manual change, an NTP correction) closes the visit
        // rather than resuming it — consistent with CaptureSessionContextPolicy
        // .resolve (CaptureSessionContext.swift:82). It does silently drop an open
        // visit, which R3-1 accepts: a WRONG visit stamping today's twenty captures
        // is the systematic error; a dropped one costs her one tap on the door.
        guard now >= context.lastActivityAt else { return .none }
        let idle = now.timeIntervalSince(context.lastActivityAt)
        guard idle <= autoEndWindow else { return .none }
        guard calendar.isDate(context.startedAt, inSameDayAs: now) else { return .none }
        return idle > staleConfirmWindow ? .stale(context) : .active(context)
    }

    static func started(_ draft: CaptureVisitDraft,
                        identity: CaptureSessionIdentity,
                        now: Date) -> CaptureSessionContext {
        CaptureSessionContext(
            identity: identity,
            startedAt: now,
            lastActivityAt: now,
            routing: CaptureRoutingMemory(
                destination: draft.kind == .sourcing ? .library : .inbox,
                projectID: draft.projectID,
                projectName: draft.projectName,
                projectRoomID: draft.projectRoomID,
                room: draft.room,
                shelf: nil),
            kind: draft.kind,
            kit: draft.kit,
            label: draft.label,
            scanRoomID: draft.scanRoomID,
            projectsInMind: draft.projectsInMind)
    }

    static func ended(_ context: CaptureSessionContext, now: Date) -> CaptureSessionContext {
        var closed = context
        closed.endedAt = now
        closed.lastActivityAt = now
        return closed
    }
}
```

In `apps/mobile/Capture/CaptureKit/CaptureKit/Session/CaptureSessionContext.swift`, replace the store's `endVisit(identity:now:)` body and add the two new members:

```swift
    public func visitState(
        identity: CaptureSessionIdentity,
        now: Date = Date(),
        calendar: Calendar = .current
    ) -> CaptureVisitState {
        let stored = defaults.data(forKey: key).flatMap {
            try? decoder.decode(CaptureSessionContext.self, from: $0)
        }
        guard let stored, stored.identity == identity else { return .none }
        return CaptureSessionContextPolicy.visitState(for: stored, now: now, calendar: calendar)
    }

    @discardableResult
    public func startVisit(
        _ draft: CaptureVisitDraft,
        identity: CaptureSessionIdentity,
        now: Date = Date()
    ) -> CaptureSessionContext {
        let context = CaptureSessionContextPolicy.started(draft, identity: identity, now: now)
        persist(context)
        return context
    }

    /// Ends the OPEN visit rather than replacing it with a fresh one: the visit
    /// keeps its `visitID` and gains an `endedAt`, so V4 (wave 4) can still read
    /// what just closed and `visitState` reads `.none` from here on.
    @discardableResult
    public func endVisit(
        identity: CaptureSessionIdentity,
        now: Date = Date()
    ) -> CaptureSessionContext {
        let open = defaults.data(forKey: key).flatMap {
            try? decoder.decode(CaptureSessionContext.self, from: $0)
        }
        guard let open, open.identity == identity, open.endedAt == nil else {
            let fresh = CaptureSessionContext(identity: identity, startedAt: now,
                                              lastActivityAt: now)
            persist(fresh)
            return fresh
        }
        let closed = CaptureSessionContextPolicy.ended(open, now: now)
        persist(closed)
        return closed
    }
```

Also amend `CaptureSessionContextPolicy.resolve(existing:identity:now:)` so an **ended** context never resumes — add `existing.endedAt == nil,` to its `guard let existing,` chain, directly beneath `existing.identity == identity,`.

```swift
// CaptureSessionContext.swift — Wave 1 shipped `stamped(onto:)`. If Task 0 found
// it ABSENT, create it here with exactly this signature.
public extension CaptureRoutingMemory {
    /// Copies the visit's routing onto a venue stamp. Wave 1 fixed the
    /// write-only projectRoomID; wave 3 keeps that behaviour unchanged.
    func stamped(onto venue: VenueStamp) -> VenueStamp
}

// Specimen.swift — all additive optionals.
public var visitKindRaw: String?
public var visitKitRaw: String?
public var visitLabel: String?
public var visitStartedAt: Date?
public var visitEndedAt: Date?
public var noteSettingRaw: String?
public var suggestedProjectID: String?
public var suggestedProjectRoomID: String?
public var suggestionBasisRaw: String?
public var suggestionConfidence: Double?
public var placementReplayPending: Bool?
/// Task 27 adds a TWELFTH column to this same block — `suggestionReasonRaw`,
/// the basis in words. It is listed there rather than here because nothing in
/// this task reads it, but the `@Model` ends the wave with twelve new optionals.

public extension Specimen {
    /// Stamps the whole visit onto a draft. `captureSessionID` already carries
    /// the visitID; this adds what the server columns need.
    func inherit(_ context: CaptureSessionContext)
    var visitKind: FieldVisitKind? { get set }
    var visitKit: FieldVisitKit? { get set }
    var noteSetting: FieldNoteSetting? { get set }
    var suggestionBasis: FieldSuggestionBasis? { get set }
    /// True when nothing has been PLACED — she never said so. The tray's scope.
    /// FC-R6 (Ruling 3): PLACEMENT is the only thing that clears this. Sync state
    /// is irrelevant — a committed capture with no project is still unplaced.
    var isUnplaced: Bool { get }
    /// FC-R6: this capture's placement has not reached the server yet.
    var placementNeedsReplay: Bool { get }
    /// Writes the FACT. Never a suggestion, never `suggested_*`.
    func place(projectID: String?, projectRoomID: String?, room: String?)
}
```

```swift
public extension Specimen {
    var visitKind: FieldVisitKind? {
        get { visitKindRaw.flatMap(FieldVisitKind.init(rawValue:)) }
        set { visitKindRaw = newValue?.rawValue }
    }
    var visitKit: FieldVisitKit? {
        get { visitKitRaw.flatMap(FieldVisitKit.init(rawValue:)) }
        set { visitKitRaw = newValue?.rawValue }
    }
    var noteSetting: FieldNoteSetting? {
        get { noteSettingRaw.flatMap(FieldNoteSetting.init(rawValue:)) }
        set { noteSettingRaw = newValue?.rawValue }
    }
    var suggestionBasis: FieldSuggestionBasis? {
        get { suggestionBasisRaw.flatMap(FieldSuggestionBasis.init(rawValue:)) }
        set { suggestionBasisRaw = newValue?.rawValue }
    }

    /// "Placed" is `venue.projectId is not null` — the same rule the server uses
    /// (`project_id IS NOT NULL`). There is no new status value, ever, and SYNC
    /// STATE IS IRRELEVANT: a capture that committed hours ago and still has no
    /// project is unplaced, and FC-R6 says it waits on Today until she files it.
    var isUnplaced: Bool {
        (venue?.projectId?.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines) ?? "").isEmpty
    }

    /// FC-R6: a capture that was placed AFTER it committed. The server learns the
    /// project on the next drain; until then the tray shows `placed · syncing`.
    var placementNeedsReplay: Bool { placementReplayPending == true }

    /// Place this capture — write the FACT. Never touches `suggested_*`.
    /// A capture that has not committed yet needs nothing more: its routing rides
    /// the FIRST commit, exactly as a capture taken inside a visit does. One that
    /// HAS committed needs the outbox to re-run `commit_field_capture`, so it is
    /// flagged for replay here and the ordinary drain does the rest.
    func place(projectID: String?, projectRoomID: String?, room: String?) {
        var stamp = venue ?? VenueStamp()
        stamp.projectId = projectID
        stamp.projectRoomId = projectRoomID
        if let room { stamp.room = room }
        venue = stamp
        if status == .committed { placementReplayPending = true }
        touch()
    }

    func inherit(_ context: CaptureSessionContext) {
        visitKind = context.kind
        visitKit = context.kit
        visitLabel = context.label
        visitStartedAt = context.kind == nil ? nil : context.startedAt
        visitEndedAt = context.endedAt
        if noteSettingRaw == nil, let kind = context.kind {
            noteSetting = CaptureVisitDraft(kind: kind, kit: context.kit).defaultNoteSetting
        }
    }
}
```

```swift
public extension FieldCapturePayload {
    struct Visit: Codable, Equatable, Sendable {
        public var id: String?          // wire key: visit.id
        public var kind: String?        // wire key: visit.kind        'site' | 'sourcing'
        public var kit: String?         // wire key: visit.kit         'walk_through' | 'trade_walk' | 'install'
        public var label: String?       // wire key: visit.label
        public var startedAt: String?   // wire key: visit.startedAt   ISO8601
        public var endedAt: String?     // wire key: visit.endedAt     ISO8601
    }
    struct Suggestion: Codable, Equatable, Sendable {
        public var projectId: String?      // wire key: suggestion.projectId
        public var projectRoomId: String?  // wire key: suggestion.projectRoomId
        public var basis: String?          // wire key: suggestion.basis
        public var confidence: Double?     // wire key: suggestion.confidence
    }
}
public var visit: FieldCapturePayload.Visit?
public var suggestion: FieldCapturePayload.Suggestion?
// Voice gains, if Task 0 found it absent:
public var noteSetting: String?          // wire key: voice.noteSetting
/// Bumped to N + 1, where N is the value Task 0 READ off merged Wave 1 and wrote
/// into the wave report (Ruling 2). On `main` before Wave 1, N is 1. Nothing in
/// this plan hardcodes 3: this task records both N and N + 1 in the wave report,
/// its test reads the constant, and the SQL test asserts the version ROUND-TRIPS.
public static let currentSchemaVersion = <N + 1>
```

⚠ **KEYS ARE THE CONTRACT** (`FieldCapturePayload.swift:9-13`): PostgREST applies no key strategy, so these property names *are* the JSON keys the SQL in Task 9 reads. Do not rename one without editing Task 9's trigger.

⚠ **`<N>` and `<N + 1>` are the two numbers Task 0 wrote into the wave report, and they are deliberately left as non-compiling tokens.** Substitute the real integers before running anything — an unsubstituted token is a compile error, which is the correct failure mode. A wrong literal would be silent, and a wire version that skips or collides with Wave 1's is invisible on both sides: `commit_field_capture` stores `capture_schema_version` with `COALESCE(…, 1)` (`00235:144`) and no reader gates on it.

```swift
    private static func buildVisit(_ s: Specimen) -> Visit? {
        guard s.visitKindRaw?.nonEmpty != nil else { return nil }
        return Visit(id: s.captureSessionID?.uuidString,
                     kind: s.visitKindRaw?.nonEmpty,
                     kit: s.visitKitRaw?.nonEmpty,
                     label: s.visitLabel?.nonEmpty,
                     startedAt: s.visitStartedAt.map(venueDateFormatter.string(from:)),
                     endedAt: s.visitEndedAt.map(venueDateFormatter.string(from:)))
    }

    private static func buildSuggestion(_ s: Specimen) -> Suggestion? {
        let project = s.suggestedProjectID?.nonEmpty
        let room = s.suggestedProjectRoomID?.nonEmpty
        let basis = s.suggestionBasisRaw?.nonEmpty
        guard project != nil || room != nil || basis != nil else { return nil }
        return Suggestion(projectId: project, projectRoomId: room,
                          basis: basis, confidence: s.suggestionConfidence)
    }
```

## Unit 3-10 · The visit/suggestion migration

> ⚠ **This unit writes SQL into `docs/design/field-companion/plans/sql/`, NOT `supabase/migrations/`.** The number is drawn from the reserved band `00530–00535` at landing by the orchestrator, who moves the file, renames it, records the row in `docs/engineering/migration-number-reservations.md`, and pushes. **No lane agent runs `supabase db push`.**

### Task 9: The migration

**Model:** sonnet

**Files:**
- Create: `docs/design/field-companion/plans/sql/005NN_field_capture_visit_and_suggestion.sql` (already authored in full by this plan — copy it verbatim from that path; it is the deliverable, not a sketch)
- Read for context: `supabase/migrations/00233_field_captures_inbox.sql:24-261` (the table, the RLS policies at `:155-186`, and the routing guard function `:195-253` + its two triggers `:255-261`), `supabase/migrations/00235_commit_field_capture_rpc.sql:85-202` (the upsert; `raw_payload` is written verbatim at `:100` on INSERT and `:184` on `ON CONFLICT DO UPDATE`, the conflict `WHERE` is `:190`, and the skipped-conflict no-op branch is `:193-202`)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `field_captures` (00233), `projects`, `project_rooms`, `commit_field_capture` (00235, **unchanged** — this migration does not touch it).
- Produces (the exact schema every later reader depends on):
```sql
field_captures.visit_id                  uuid                       -- no FK, no visits table
field_captures.visit_kind                text  CHECK IN ('site','sourcing')
field_captures.visit_kit                 text  CHECK IN ('walk_through','trade_walk','install')
field_captures.visit_label               text
field_captures.visit_started_at          timestamptz
field_captures.visit_ended_at            timestamptz
field_captures.suggested_project_id      uuid  REFERENCES projects(id)      ON DELETE SET NULL
field_captures.suggested_project_room_id uuid  REFERENCES project_rooms(id) ON DELETE SET NULL
field_captures.suggestion_basis          text  CHECK IN ('visit','scan','proximity','venue','calendar','transcript')
field_captures.suggestion_confidence     numeric(3,2) CHECK BETWEEN 0 AND 1
INDEX idx_field_captures_visit ON field_captures (project_id, visit_id, created_at DESC) WHERE project_id IS NOT NULL
FUNCTION public.field_captures_project_visit_columns() RETURNS trigger   -- SECURITY INVOKER, TOTAL
TRIGGER  trg_field_captures_visit_projection BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE ON field_captures
```

⚠ **`idx_field_captures_visit` is partial on `project_id IS NOT NULL` and that is correct.** It serves wave 4's `useProjectVisits` read (`wave-4-plan.md:1334`) — "every capture on this project, grouped by visit, newest first" — and a visit is only ever listed on a project spread, so a row with no `project_id` can never satisfy that query. **Nothing in wave 3 reads it**; the unplaced tray is device-side SwiftData. The migration says so in a comment so a future reader does not "fix" a predicate that has no `project_id IS NULL` caller.

> **Ruling 1 (2026-08-24): the projection never raises.** The trigger body parses every value defensively — unknown `visit_kind` / `visit_kit` / `suggestion_basis` → NULL, unparsable uuid / timestamp / numeric → NULL, out-of-range confidence → NULL — and records each drop in `raw_payload->'visit_projection_errors'` as `{key, sqlstate, at}`. **The named CHECKs stay** as the invariant for direct writers; the trigger is what makes them untrippable from a device payload.
>
> This is not defensive decoration. `commit_field_capture`'s upsert sits **outside** its own safe-harbor block (`00235:223-299` wraps only the library branch), so a cast or CHECK failure in this trigger escapes the RPC on the library path and has no harbor at all on the inbox path. Patina Field is offline-first and **retries**: a capture whose payload trips one of these would raise the same error forever, never sync, and hold the queue behind it. Six payload classes did exactly that before this fix — measured against the local database, all six raised (`22007`, `23514` ×3, `22003`, `22P02`) and all six now commit. Task 10's assertion group 12 is that proof, kept.

⚠ **Deviation from spec §9.3, deliberate and priced.** §9.3 lists the columns but names no writer, and the only column-writing path is `commit_field_capture` — the one object §9.0 flags as having **two live authors**, where "whichever lane lands second silently reverts the other." This migration therefore stays off that object and projects the columns from `raw_payload` in a BEFORE trigger instead. `commit_field_capture` already stores the entire payload in `raw_payload` on both the INSERT and the `ON CONFLICT DO UPDATE` path (`00235:100`, `:184`), so the projection is exact, idempotent, and cannot collide with Phase 3's 00516 or wave 1's routing migration. Raise this with the FC-R18 owner before the push; if the ruling instead folds the reads into `commit_field_capture`, the trigger is dropped and the reads move — the column list, the CHECK names and this task's SQL test are unaffected either way.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

Take the file authored at `docs/design/field-companion/plans/sql/field_capture_visit_test.sql` **verbatim** — all twelve assertion groups are in it, including the three the review added. Three of them are worth reading before you run anything, because each replaces something that looked like proof and was not:

**Group 4b — the arm the design's claim actually rests on.** Group 4 uses a *nonexistent* uuid, which exercises the FK arm. 4b commits, as designer A, a suggestion naming a project that **exists and belongs to designer B**, and asserts no RAISE with `suggested_project_id IS NULL`. The projection's `EXISTS` probe runs under the caller's RLS (`commit_field_capture` is `SECURITY INVOKER`, `00235:61`), so a project she cannot see is treated as absent. That — not the FK — is why "suggestions are not facts" is structurally true.

**Group 10 — read the names, do not assert about literals.** The earlier draft compared two string constants (`'trg_field_captures_guard_insert' < 'trg_field_captures_visit_projection'`), which is true regardless of the database and proves neither trigger exists nor that either is named that. The shipped file `SELECT`s both `tgname`s from `pg_trigger` and compares the retrieved values. The property is real — the observed firing order on the live table is `trg_field_captures_guard_insert → trg_field_captures_guard_update → trg_field_captures_updated_at → trg_field_captures_visit_projection` — which is exactly why a fake proof of it is worse than none.

**Group 12 — the projection never raises (Ruling 1).** One commit per malformed class: unparsable timestamp (`22007`), unknown kit (`23514`), unknown basis (`23514`), confidence 1.4 (`23514`), confidence 12.5 (`22003`, `numeric(3,2)` overflow *before* the CHECK), non-uuid visit id (`22P02`) — plus one on the **library** destination, the branch whose safe harbor does not cover the upsert. Each asserts the commit **succeeds**, the offending column is NULL, **the rest of the envelope still projects**, and the drop is recorded under its key in `raw_payload->'visit_projection_errors'`. All six were measured raising against the pre-fix body and passing against the shipped one.

```swift
//  VisitDoorTests.swift
//  CaptureTests
//
//  V0 — the door (spec §7.3). Three taps and ≤8 s, IN AIRPLANE MODE.

import Foundation
import Testing
@testable import CaptureKit

@MainActor
struct VisitDoorTests {

    private let owner = CaptureOwnerIdentity(userID: "u1", workspaceID: "w1")!

    private final class StubProjects: ProjectsService, @unchecked Sendable {
        var list: [FieldProject] = []
        var detail: [String: FieldProjectDetail] = [:]
        var offline = false
        struct Offline: Error {}
        func listProjects() async throws -> [FieldProject] {
            if offline { throw Offline() }
            return list
        }
        func projectDetail(id: String) async throws -> FieldProjectDetail {
            if offline { throw Offline() }
            guard let d = detail[id] else { throw Offline() }
            return d
        }
    }

    private func makeCache(_ service: StubProjects) throws -> CaptureProjectCache {
        let store = try CaptureStore.inMemory()
        return CaptureProjectCache(store: store, projects: service)
    }

        let service = StubProjects()

        let harbor = FieldProject(id: "p2", name: "Harbor loft", status: "active")
        service.list = [maple, harbor]
        service.detail["p1"] = FieldProjectDetail(
            project: maple,
            specRooms: [FieldProjectRoom(id: "sr1", name: "Living"),
                        FieldProjectRoom(id: "sr2", name: "Dining")],
            rooms: [FieldProjectRoom(id: "r1", name: "Living")])
        return service
    }
```

Create `apps/mobile/Capture/CaptureKit/CaptureKit/Work/FieldVisitDoorModel.swift`:

```swift
//  FieldVisitDoorModel.swift
//  CaptureKit
//
//  V0 — the door (spec §7.3). "Where are you today?" answered once, offline,
//  in three taps. The model lives in CaptureKit so it is unit-testable; the
//  sheet is a thin renderer over it.

import Foundation
import Observation

@MainActor
@Observable
public final class FieldVisitDoorModel {
    private let cache: CaptureProjectCache
    private let owner: CaptureOwnerIdentity
    private let existing: CaptureVisitState
    private var allProjects: [CaptureProjectSnapshot] = []
    private var selectedDetail: CaptureProjectSnapshot?

    public var kind: FieldVisitKind = .site
    public var query: String = ""
    public var selectedProjectID: String?
    public var selectedRoom: FieldVisitRoomOption?
    public var kit: FieldVisitKit?
    public var venueName: String = ""
    public private(set) var projectsInMind: [String] = []
    public private(set) var isOffline = false

    public init(cache: CaptureProjectCache,
                owner: CaptureOwnerIdentity,
                existing: CaptureVisitState = .none) {
        self.cache = cache
        self.owner = owner
        self.existing = existing
        if let open = existing.context {
            kind = open.kind ?? .site
            kit = open.kit
            selectedProjectID = open.routing.projectID
            venueName = open.kind == .sourcing ? (open.label ?? "") : ""
            projectsInMind = open.projectsInMind
        }
    }

    public var projects: [CaptureProjectSnapshot] {
        CaptureProjectCachePolicy.filter(allProjects, query: query)
    }

    public var offlineCaption: String? {
        isOffline ? CaptureProjectCachePolicy.offlineCaption(cachedCount: allProjects.count) : nil
    }

    public var roomOptions: [FieldVisitRoomOption] {
        guard let selectedDetail else { return [] }
        return [.wholeHouse] + FieldVisitRoomMerge.merge(specRooms: selectedDetail.specRooms,
                                                         rooms: selectedDetail.rooms)
    }

    public var scanLaneCaption: String? {
        FieldVisitRoomMerge.scanLaneCaption(Array(roomOptions.dropFirst()))
    }

    public var isChangingAnOpenVisit: Bool { existing.isVisit }

    public var primaryTitle: String { isChangingAnOpenVisit ? "Change" : "Start visit" }

    public var canStart: Bool {
        switch kind {
        case .site:
            return selectedProjectID?.isEmpty == false
        case .sourcing:
            return !venueName.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines).isEmpty
        }
    }

    public func load() async {
        let refreshed = await cache.refreshList(owner: owner)
        isOffline = !refreshed
        allProjects = cache.snapshots(owner: owner)
        if let selectedProjectID {
            await select(projectID: selectedProjectID)
        }
    }

    public func select(projectID: String) async {
        if selectedProjectID != projectID { selectedRoom = nil }
        selectedProjectID = projectID
        let refreshed = await cache.refreshDetail(projectID: projectID, owner: owner)
        if !refreshed { isOffline = true }
        allProjects = cache.snapshots(owner: owner)
        selectedDetail = allProjects.first { $0.id == projectID }
    }

    public func toggleProjectInMind(_ id: String) {
        if let index = projectsInMind.firstIndex(of: id) {
            projectsInMind.remove(at: index)
        } else if projectsInMind.count < CaptureSessionContext.maxProjectsInMind {
            projectsInMind.append(id)
        }
    }

    public func draft() -> CaptureVisitDraft? {
        guard canStart else { return nil }
        switch kind {
        case .site:
            guard let projectID = selectedProjectID,
                  let project = allProjects.first(where: { $0.id == projectID }) else { return nil }
            let room = selectedRoom.flatMap { $0.isWholeHouse ? nil : $0 }
            return CaptureVisitDraft(
                kind: .site,
                kit: kit,
                label: project.name,
                projectID: projectID,
                projectName: project.name,
                projectRoomID: room?.projectRoomID,
                scanRoomID: room?.scanRoomID,
                room: room?.name)
        case .sourcing:
            return CaptureVisitDraft(
                kind: .sourcing,
                kit: kit,
                label: venueName.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines),
                projectsInMind: projectsInMind)
        }
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the implementation**

```swift
public extension FieldVisitDoorModel {
    /// Never overwrites a typed venue — a GPS guess is a courtesy, not a fact.
    func prefillVenue(from location: any LocationService) async {
        guard venueName.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines).isEmpty else { return }
        guard let placemark = await location.currentVenue()?.placemarkName else { return }
        venueName = placemark
    }

    var offeredKits: [FieldVisitKit] { FieldVisitKit.allCases }

    /// FC-R11: the kit carries the consent default, and wave 3 ships ONLY that
    /// posture — the four-way pill row that tunes the shutter is wave 4.
    var consentPosture: FieldNoteSetting {
        CaptureVisitDraft(kind: kind, kit: kit).defaultNoteSetting
    }

    var projectsInMindIsFull: Bool {
        projectsInMind.count >= CaptureSessionContext.maxProjectsInMind
    }
}
```
