- Produces (spec §14 — exactly these names and properties, no others):
```swift
public enum FieldVisitTelemetry {
    public static func visitStart(kind: FieldVisitKind, kit: FieldVisitKit?,
                                  offline: Bool) -> (name: String, properties: [String: String])
    public static func visitEnd(duration: TimeInterval, captures: Int, notes: Int,
                                scans: Int, unplaced: Int) -> (name: String, properties: [String: String])
    public static func stalePrompt(answer: String) -> (name: String, properties: [String: String])
    public static func suggestionShown(_ s: CaptureSuggestion) -> (name: String, properties: [String: String])
    public static func suggestionAccepted(_ s: CaptureSuggestion) -> (name: String, properties: [String: String])
    public static func capturePlaced(basis: String, hasRoom: Bool) -> (name: String, properties: [String: String])
    public static let captureUnplaced: (name: String, properties: [String: String])
}
```

⚠ **`suggestion_confidence` must not appear in any event property** — Principle 4 forbids the number on any *designer* surface, and a telemetry property that leaks into a dashboard someone screenshots into a design review is the exact path by which a surface talks itself into showing one. Order the tray with it; never emit it.

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the implementation**

Create `apps/mobile/Capture/CaptureKit/CaptureKit/Analytics/FieldVisitTelemetry.swift`:

```swift
//  FieldVisitTelemetry.swift
//  CaptureKit
//
//  Spec §14. Field had never sent a single analytics event before wave 1, so
//  every tap count and target in this program is unfalsifiable without these.
//  The names and property keys ARE the contract — a dashboard reads them.
//
//  suggestion_confidence is deliberately absent from every property bag:
//  Principle 4 forbids the number on a designer surface, and a telemetry
//  property is exactly how one gets there.

import Foundation

public enum FieldVisitTelemetry {
    public typealias Event = (name: String, properties: [String: String])

    public static func visitStart(kind: FieldVisitKind, kit: FieldVisitKit?,
                                  offline: Bool) -> Event {
        ("visit.start", ["kind": kind.rawValue,
                         "kit": kit?.rawValue ?? "none",
                         "offline": offline ? "true" : "false"])
    }

    public static func visitEnd(duration: TimeInterval, captures: Int, notes: Int,
                                scans: Int, unplaced: Int) -> Event {
        ("visit.end", ["duration_min": String(Int((duration / 60).rounded())),
                       "captures": String(captures),
                       "notes": String(notes),
                       "scans": String(scans),
                       "unplaced": String(unplaced)])
    }

    public static func stalePrompt(answer: String) -> Event {
        ("visit.stale_prompt", ["answer": answer])
    }

    public static func suggestionShown(_ suggestion: CaptureSuggestion) -> Event {
        ("suggestion.shown", ["basis": suggestion.basis.rawValue])
    }

    public static func suggestionAccepted(_ suggestion: CaptureSuggestion) -> Event {
        ("suggestion.accepted", ["basis": suggestion.basis.rawValue])
    }

    public static func capturePlaced(basis: String, hasRoom: Bool) -> Event {
        ("capture.placed", ["basis": basis, "has_room": hasRoom ? "true" : "false"])
    }

    public static let captureUnplaced: Event = ("capture.unplaced", [:])
}

public extension CaptureAnalytics {
    func emit(_ event: FieldVisitTelemetry.Event) {
        self.event(event.name, event.properties)
    }
}
```

Then replace the inline `analytics.event("visit.start", …)` / `"visit.stale_prompt"` / `"suggestion.shown"` / `"suggestion.accepted"` / `"capture.placed"` / `"capture.unplaced"` calls written in Tasks 13, 15, 21 and 27 with `analytics.emit(FieldVisitTelemetry.…)`, and add `visit.end` at **all THREE** end-visit call sites, computing its five counts from the tray's own arrays:

| # | Call site | Written by |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | `V0VisitSheet`'s destructive **End visit** footer button | Task 13 |
| 2 | `V1SessionTrayScreen.endVisit()`, reached from the tray footer's primary | Task 25 |
| 3 | **`RootView.handleCompanionAction`'s `case "visit.end"`** | Task 17 |

⚠ **Site 3 is the one that gets missed**, and it is reachable from *every non-camera screen* through the collapsed Companion strip — Invariant V's own carrier. Miss it and §14's `visit.end` duration/count metric silently under-counts every visit she ends from the strip, which is the cheapest way to end one. `WorkDashboardScreen`'s stale-prompt **End visit** goes through `model.refreshVisit()` and is the same act; emit there too if it does not already route through site 1 or 2.

⚠ **`visit.end`'s five counts need the visit's captures**, which `RootView` does not hold. Read them the same way `WorkDashboardModel.refreshVisit()` does — `store.session(visitID:owner:)` plus `store.unfiled(owner:)` — before calling `endVisit`, since after it the context is closed and `visitState` reads `.none`.

### Task 33: The device pass — a real eight-minute walk at a real address, half of it in airplane mode

**Model:** Kody (a human, on a physical iPhone). No agent may mark this task complete.

**Files:** none. The deliverable is the filled-in table below, pasted into the wave report.

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: a signed device build (never `CODE_SIGNING_ALLOWED=NO` — a sim-walk harness build must not be installed for a walk).
- Produces: the exercised/not-exercised record, and any bug list that follows from it.

⚠ Simulator runs prove none of this. `AVFoundation` and `CoreLocation` are mocked on the simulator (`AppContainer.swift:14-19`), which is exactly the half of wave 3 that matters: the venue stamp, the learned centroid, the microphone, and the offline door.

- [ ] **Step 1: Prepare**

```bash
cd /Users/kody/Code/patina-merged/apps/mobile/Capture
ruby scripts/generate_project.rb
xcodebuild -project Capture.xcodeproj -scheme Capture -destination 'generic/platform=iOS' archive
```

Install the signed build on a physical iPhone. Confirm before leaving: signed in as a real designer with **at least two** real projects, one of which has both `project_rooms` and `public.rooms` entries, and one of which has **no registered client** (so the `public.rooms` lane is empty — that is the case §9.7 says degrades silently).

- [ ] **Step 2: Walk it — eight minutes, at a real address, airplane mode for at least four**

The route, in order:
1. Cold launch with no visit → lands on **Today**.
2. **Turn on airplane mode.** Start a visit: `+ Start a visit` → project → room → **Start visit**. Time it and count the taps.
3. Shutter a photo → hold the card's mic → speak ten seconds → release → **Save**.
4. Swipe to **VOICE**, tap to start, talk for **three minutes while walking between rooms**, lock the phone mid-note, unlock, tap **Stop**.
5. Open **F1 Site scan** and confirm it collapsed to one line.
6. Background the app for **35 minutes** (lunch), reopen → confirm the *"Still at …?"* prompt.
7. **Turn airplane mode off.** Watch the queue drain.
8. Take one capture with **no visit open** and confirm it is born with a suggestion, in words.
9. Repeat step 2 on the **client-less** project and read the honest expansion out loud.
10. End the visit from the tray.

| 21 | The word "Inbox" appeared nowhere she can read | ☐ yes ☐ no | |
| 22 | `visit.start`, `visit.end` and `capture.placed` arrived in PostHog | ☐ yes ☐ no | |
| 23 | The 12-hour auto-end | ☐ **not exercised** — an eight-minute walk cannot reach it | verify separately, or accept it on the unit test |
| 24 | The never-across-a-calendar-day rule | ☐ **not exercised** — needs an overnight | verify separately, or accept it on the unit test |
| 25 | The 20-minute / 24-segment cap | ☐ **not exercised** unless a 20-minute note was recorded | |

- [ ] **Step 4: Report**

Paste the completed table into the wave report. **Any row marked "no" that is not already in the not-exercised set is a wave-3 bug**, and it is listed by name with a reproduction before the wave is called done. Rows 23–25 stay "not exercised" honestly — they are covered by `VisitContextTests` and `VoiceModeTests`, and saying so is the whole point of the column.

- [ ] **Step 5: Commit**

No commit. The device pass is a gate, not a change. When it is clean, the orchestrator fast-forward-merges `feat/field-companion-w3` into `main`, draws the migration number from `00530–00535`, moves `docs/design/field-companion/plans/sql/005NN_field_capture_visit_and_suggestion.sql` into `supabase/migrations/` under its drawn name, records the row in `docs/engineering/migration-number-reservations.md`, and pushes.

---

## Rollback

No flag; the gate is the build. Rollback is the prior build. The migration is **additive and inert** without a Field build that writes the visit payload keys: with no `visit` envelope in `raw_payload`, the trigger writes nothing, and every new column stays NULL. Dropping the trigger (`DROP TRIGGER trg_field_captures_visit_projection ON field_captures;`) restores exactly the pre-wave behaviour without touching a column or a row.

## What this wave does NOT do

- **No V4 visit-review screen** — that is wave 4 (`CaptureScreenID.v4VisitReview` exists from wave 2 and stays unused here).
- **No portal surface.** The Visits block, the margin row, the time entry and the punch verb are all wave 4.
- **No four-way kit pill row on C1.** Wave 3 ships only the kit's **consent posture** (FC-R11); the pills that tune the shutter land in wave 4 with the verbs they tune.
- **No `CLVisit`, no `EventKit`, no Always-location.** The learned centroid replaces them as the first suggestion source; wave 5 decides whether either is still wanted.
- **No local notification for the unplaced count.** FC-R6's owed mechanism is named in the spec's §16.16 as an accepted limitation; wave 5's Live Activity is the candidate carrier. The tray and Today's unplaced line are correct and permanent (Ruling 3) — what is missing is the *prompt* to open them.
- **No re-placement from S1.** `S1AssignVenueScreen` persists routing on its own path and cannot move a capture that has already committed; `Specimen.place(…)` and the outbox replay are wired into the **tray** only. That is a pre-existing gap (§7.6 re-homes S1 as the per-capture correction screen without changing its persistence), not one this wave introduces — but a designer who corrects a synced capture from S1 will not see the change reach the studio. Wave 4 owns it.
- **No `route_field_capture` on any wave-3 path.** It is `commit_field_capture(…, 'library', …)` with a hardcoded destination (`00235:330-338`), so it is a *promotion* verb, not a placement verb. Placement re-runs `commit_field_capture` with the destination unchanged.
- **No server-side visit close.** Flow 7's `visit_ended_at` is a device-side nicety; a real close costs a narrow update RPC and is priced when it is wanted.
